# Duel core: documented functions (`duel_core`)

Reference table generated in the documentation round of 2026-10-31 and extended in the round of 2026-11-03. Each row is a function that reached the `documented` stage (`docs/PROGRESS.md`): its prototype, purpose, return value, side effects and callers, read from the Ghidra decompilation with the names of that round. The same text is the function's plate comment in Ghidra (`re/documented*.tsv`, applied by `tools/ghidra/ApplySignatures.java`). How the subsystem works as a whole is in [`../duel_core.md`](../duel_core.md); pointers to structures are typed `void *` and the structure is named in the summary.

Functions: 978 (rel_duel_draw 75, rel_duel_eng 896, rel_duel_mgr 7).


## `rel_duel_draw`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x0989d4c8` | `void duel_core_init_view_side_map(void)` | Builds the side maps of the draw module so that the local side is view side 0: with l = thunk_duel_core_is_local_side(1), sets the engine-to-view map 0x098e9aa0 = (l != 0), 0x098e9aa4 = (l == 0), and copies them to the view-to-engine map 0x098e9aa8, 0x098e9aac. No return value. Side effects: the four map words. Callers: 1 (ui_duel_draw_begin). |
| `0x0989d534` | `int duel_core_engine_side_to_view(int side)` | rel_duel_draw: returns the view side of engine side from the table 0x098e9aa0[side]. No side effects. Callers: 46 (ui_cmd_show_chain_link_activated, ui_duel_draw_begin, ui_cmd_show_turn_change, ui_cmd_move_list_card, ...). |

| `0x09dab210` | `int duel_core_summon_prepare(void)` | Preparation stage of the summon procedure: dispatches on the card id of the summon record (0x09e290bc, id +0x04) to a card-specific routine or the generic one and converts its result. Default: Synchro -> duel_core_summon_prepare_synchro; extra-deck procedure monsters -> duel_core_summon_prepare_contact_fusion; a procedure summon -> tributes; Elemental Hero -> duel_core_summon_step_necroshade; Ancient Gear -> duel_core_summon_step_gear_castle_tribute; Dinosaur (race 9) -> duel_core_summon_step_big_evolution_pill; otherwise duel_core_summon_select_tributes. A negative routine result sets record flag +0x34 bit 3 (cancelled), posts UI event 0x2b and returns 1; result 1 re-initialises the record with duel_core_init_procedure_summon(side, ref, position, tribute count) when +0x34 bit 1 is set, else duel_core_init_normal_summon(side, ref, tribute count, position == Attack), and returns 1; otherwise returns 0 (call again). Side effects: summon record, LP, hand, UI events through the routines. Callers: 1 (duel_core_run_summon_procedure). |

| `0x09dac78c` | `void duel_core_queue_summoned_card_triggers(uint event)` | After a summon, queues the summoned card's own "when summoned" trigger: does nothing unless the card in the record's slot (summon record 0x09e290bc: controller bit 1, slot bits 2-5) is face-up (slot +0x08). Builds a 32-bit summary: bits 0-8 card instance (record bits 13-21), bit 9 controller, bits 10-13 slot, bit 14 position, bit 15 face-up, bit 16 summoner differs from controller, bit 17 proper summon (type +0x06 bit 0), bits 18-19 distinct tributes (+0x0b, max 3), bits 20-23 source location. Per event (6 Normal/Tribute Summon, 7 Flip Summon, 8 Special Summon) per-card id lists decide whether the card triggers (tribute-count conditions, Gladiator Beast summoner +0x08, proper-summon bit, summoned from the Graveyard or hand ...); event 7 gives code 2 for cards with a FLIP effect (thunk_card_db_get_flip_kind). A positive card calls duel_core_chain_queue_trigger(card id \| slot << 16 \| controller << 31 \| event << 25 \| 0x200000, instance, summary); for a FLIP card whose trigger was queued it also posts command 0x62 (card id). Cards 0x190a and 0x1488 queue their own triggers (0x190a once per set bit 7-9 of its player-slot effect value, which it removes). Card 0x1a1f only gets a kind-2 marker. No return value. Side effects: trigger queue, command 0x62 and marker posts. Callers: 3 (duel_core_summon_step_special, duel_core_summon_step_normal, duel_core_summon_step_flip). |

Step 0/1: a position of 2 ("ask") is taken from the answer 0x09e2d248 (Attack also sets face-up bit 6); a face-up summon posts command 0x61 (a = ref \| 0x8000, b = position, c = tribute count +0x0a) and player flag 16; clears the scratch ring 0x09e28e50 entries at index 0x09e28e4c and +1 and the distinct-tribute count +0x0b; per tribute (refs at +0x0c) posts command 0x49 (slot), builds a slot mask (bit slot + side x 16) for duel_core_tribute_monsters, and accumulates per-card tribute facts into the ring (Attribute, Lightsworn, roid, Batteryman, ATK/DEF sums; the tribute's side\|slot word at record +0x20). Step 2: with no zone accepted by duel_core_filter_summon_zone the card is sent to the Graveyard from its location (banished, extra deck, Deck or hand) and 1 is returned; otherwise zone selection with filter 0x09dac660. Step 3: target slot from 0x09e2d284 into record bits 2-5, posts command 0x50 (a = card id, b = position bit << 15 \| face_up << 14 \| slot << 1 \| controller, c = ref << 1 \| uses-Normal-Summon flag +0x34 bit 2), then card-specific bookkeeping that remembers the tributes (duel_core_post_set_slot_value or duel_core_post_add_marker for 0x1c62, 0x1ed8, 0x1dbc, 0x1a30, 0x164f, 5000 ...). Step 4: face-up posts command 0x2b (card id, slot, 0) and goes to 0x0a; a Set posts command 0x44 (slot, type +0x06, tribute count) and goes to 0x15. Steps 0x0a-0x0e: duel_core_summon_raise_trigger(0x20) (negation window and event 0x20). Step 0x14: done if the slot is empty; a slot carrying effect 0x12fd loses that marker and slot flag 3 and ends; otherwise command 0x44 (count + 1 for summoning card 0x19b2). Step 0x15: after duel_effects_update_negated_slots_default, duel_core_queue_summon_event_triggers(6 face-up / 9 set). Step 0x16: cards 0x13e8/0x133d summoned face-up destroy themselves (duel_core_request_destroy_slot_by_card in a timing batch); otherwise duel_core_enforce_slot_controller, re-locates the card and calls duel_core_queue_summoned_card_triggers(6). Returns 1 when finished, 0 while running. Side effects: summon record, answer flag 0x09e2d258, ring 0x09e28e50, commands and requests above. Callers: 1 (duel_core_run_summon_procedure). |
| `0x09dafc90` | `int duel_core_summon_step_flip(void)` | Execution stage of summon kind 2 (Flip Summon), on summon record sub-step +0x36. Step 0 posts command 0x61 (ref \| 0x8000, 0, 4) and player flag 16, then either command 0x34 (slot, 1, 2) when the monster is face-down in Attack Position (record byte +0x39 = 0) or command 0x33 (slot, 1, 1) when it is in Defense Position (+0x39 = 1). Step 1 posts command 0x2b (card id, slot, 1) and goes to 0x0a. Steps 0x0a-0x0e: duel_core_summon_raise_trigger(0x21). Step 0x14: done if the slot is empty, otherwise command 0x44 (slot, type +0x06, tribute count +0x0a). Step 0x15: after duel_effects_update_negated_slots_default, slot flag 21 (duel_core_post_set_slot_flag) and duel_core_queue_summon_event_triggers(7). Step 0x16: a Level <= 2 monster while 0x1255 is active is destroyed (duel_core_post_announce_card, duel_core_request_destroy_slot_by_card in a timing batch, by the opponent when the opponent controls 0x1255) and 1 is returned; otherwise duel_core_enforce_slot_controller and duel_core_queue_summoned_card_triggers(7). Returns 1 when finished, 0 while running. Side effects: commands 0x61/0x34/0x33/0x2b/0x44, player and slot flags, trigger queue, destruction request. Callers: 1 (duel_core_run_summon_procedure). |
| `0x09db00f8` | `int duel_core_summon_step_special(void)` | Execution stage of summon kind 4 (Special Summon by effect), also entered from kind 5, a state machine on the summon record sub-step +0x36 (0x09e290f2). Sub-step 0: command 11 (enter Standby Phase). 1: runs the 102 (function, argument) upkeep checks at 0x09e0155c (first entry duel_core_standby_destroy_spell_trap_if_hand_below_5) until all report done. 2: timing event 2; 3: back to 1 while new events were queued (0x09e28e40), else 100. 100: re-runs the checks with 0x09e28f84 = 1, then walks both sides' slots 0-9 (flag bit 27 clear): 0x1d86 (own) banishes from the Graveyard (yes/no 0x8c, card list mode 6, 0x8e) or goes to the Graveyard, 0x161b removes a counter (0x82-0x84) or is destroyed, the LP-maintenance cards 0x1bb2/0x151a/0x13f9/0x13f4/0x1360/0x134a/0x1381/0xf4a ask to pay duel_effects_get_standby_lp_cost (0x6e, ai_answer_maintenance_cost for the CPU; 0x6f pays or destroys), 0x144a/0x1358 ask for a Tribute (0x78-0x7a) or are destroyed; then the turn player's 0x1e25 and 0x1cca/0x1afc/0x168c-0x1692 pay their cost (0x74; a 0x16a2 link also makes a player pay again) or are destroyed when LP is too low, Archfiends under 0x169f asking first (0x73) and getting marker 0x168c instead of being destroyed. 0x96 -> 200: per-turn counters: 0x1b50 marker removal, 0x14ac and 0xff9 link upkeep, duel_core_update_pumpking, turn counters of spell/traps 0x1ee4/0x1a7e/0x15a0, 0x1aa9 (destroyed at 2), 0x1de2/0x1dd1/0x1c91/0x187c/0x17a1 (destroyed at 1), 0x15ee, 0x159c (destroyed at 1, its banished card returned with command 0x3e), 0x180f/0x131a/0x130d, and player-list records 0x12c8 (4 turns) and 0x1811 (2 turns, then hand shuffle) post command 0x3a countdowns and, at 0, return the banished card (command 0x3e) and raise timing event 0x25. Returns 1 when finished (sub-step past 200), else 0. Side effects: commands 0x0b/0x3a/0x3e, LP payments, destroy/send/banish requests, slot flag bit 27, prompts, timing events 2 and 0x25. No direct caller (turn step table 0x09e01da0 entry 2). |
| `0x09de3b78` | `int duel_core_step_turn_end(void)` | Last turn step (table 0x09e01da0), sub-step 0x09e2d214. Sub-step 0: posts command 3 (end-of-turn cleanup) for the turn side 0x09e2d1d8. Sub-step 1: in duel mode 2 (0x09e2d1f4) when the turn counter 0x09e2d1e0 equals 0x09e2d1e4 calls duel_core_declare_turn_limit_loss(local side); else when the counter equals 0x09e2d1e4 + 3 and the two sides' LP (block +0x00) differ, decides the duel for the side with more LP: result 0x09e2d1f0 = 1 when that is the local side (0x09e02994), else 2, win reason 0x0b in that side's +0x30 and in 0x09e02cb4, 0x09e2d1d4 = 1; otherwise posts command 4 (next turn). Always returns 0. Side effects: posted commands, the duel-result globals. No direct caller (turn step table 0x09e01da0). |
| `0x09de3d40` | `int duel_core_net_run_peer_target_choice(void)` | Link-duel request run for the peer, state 0x09e289ac: 0 clears 0x09e28f6c, 0x09e28f70, 0x09e28f74 and goes to 1; 1 sets 0x09e28e44 to the link at 0x09e28cb4 + 0x09e28e38 x 0x18 and calls duel_effects_call_handler_14(link, previous) (previous = the link 0x18 bytes lower, null when 0x09e28e38 < 1 or its card id is 0); a non-zero result stores (result > 0) in bit 0 of link byte +4 and advances to 2; returns 0. State 2 sends the 0x18-byte link with duel_core_net_send_command(0x19, -1, link, 0x18) and returns 1; any other state returns 1. Side effects: the state word, the link's +4 bit 0, 0x09e28e44, the network packet. Callers: 1 (duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests). |
| `0x09de3e5c` | `int duel_core_net_run_peer_resolve(void)` | Link-duel request run for the peer, state 0x09e289b0: 0 clears 0x09e28f6c, 0x09e28f70, 0x09e28f64, 0x09e28f68, sets 0x09e28f60 = 0x80, calls duel_core_clear_deck_shuffle_flags and goes to 1; 1 sets 0x09e28e44 to the link at 0x09e28cb4 + 0x09e28e38 x 0x18 and stores duel_effects_resolve(link, previous) in 0x09e28f60 (previous = the link 0x18 bytes lower, null when 0x09e28e38 < 1), advancing to 2 when it returns 0; returns 0. State 2 sends duel_core_net_send_command(0x17, -1, ...) with the link (0x18 bytes) or, when a previous link exists, both links (0x30 bytes from the previous one) and returns 1; other states return 1. Side effects: the state word, 0x09e28f60-0x09e28f70, 0x09e28e44, player flags bit 3, those of duel_effects_resolve, the packet. Callers: 1 (duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests). |
| `0x09de3f88` | `int duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests(void)` | Link duel: runs one pending peer request per call. When the activation-step flag 0x09e2898c is set it works on the chain link at index 0x09e28e38 (0x09e28cb4 + index x 0x18; previous link or NULL): state 0x09e289a8 0 clears 0x09e28f6c/0x09e28f74 and goes to 1; 1 makes it the current link (0x09e28e44) and calls duel_effects_call_activation_step(link, previous), and on a non-zero result sets link byte +3 bit 7 when positive and advances; 2 sends the 24-byte link to the peer (duel_core_net_send_command(0x1b, -1, link, 0x18)) and clears the flag. Otherwise it runs, in order, the first set request: 0x09e28984 duel_core_net_run_peer_target_choice, 0x09e28970 duel_core_net_answer_response_window, 0x09e28994 duel_core_net_run_peer_resolve, clearing that flag when the worker returns non-zero. Returns 0 when nothing was pending, else 1. Side effects: the request flags and state, the link, the packet send. Callers: 1 (duel_core_poll_net_peer_requests). |
| `0x09de413c` | `int duel_core_poll_net_peer_requests(void)` | Runs duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests() (the requests the local side runs for the link-duel peer) and returns 0. Side effects: those of duel_core_net_run_peer_link_requests. Callers: 1 (duel_core_run_turn_flow). |

## `rel_duel_mgr`

| Address | Prototype | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x08ea9cd4` | `int duel_core_is_local_default_rules_duel(void)` | Returns 1 for a duel that is not a network duel (thunk_system_get_net_active_flag() == 0) and whose set-up uses the default rules (duel_core_setup_has_default_rules: LP mode +0x196 and time limit +0x197 both 0); otherwise 0. No side effects. Callers: 2 (ui_duel_result_init, ui_duel_result_draw_summary_page). |
| `0x08ea9d14` | `int duel_core_setup_has_default_rules(void)` | Returns 1 when the duel set-up block (thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer()) has LP mode +0x196 == 0 (8000 LP) and time limit +0x197 == 0, otherwise 0. No side effects. Callers: 2 (duel_core_is_local_default_rules_duel, ui_duel_result_init). |
| `0x08ea9ef0` | `int duel_core_get_setup_start_lp(int side)` | Returns the starting Life Points from the duel set-up block (thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer()) LP mode byte +0x196: 1 -> 2000, 2 -> 4000, 3 -> 12000, 4 -> 16000, 5 -> the custom value u32 +0x198 (side 0) or +0x19c (side != 0), any other value -> 8000. No side effects. Callers: 1 (duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel). |
| `0x08ea9f68` | `bool duel_core_is_player_on_second_side(void)` | Scans the duellist records (0x1ac bytes each at +0x1a4 of the duel set-up buffer from thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer; 2 records, or 4 when byte +0x192 is 2 = tag) for character u16 +0x00 == 1 (the player): records 0 and 2 set bit 0, records 1 and 3 set bit 1. Returns true when only bit 1 is set (the player duels only on the second side). No side effects. Callers: 1 (duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel). |
| `0x08eaa878` | `void duel_core_reset_turn_timers(void)` | Resets the four duelist timers (the first four ints of the duel session block returned by thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_tail_block, +0x00..+0x0c) to limit - 1, where the limit comes from the duel set-up buffer (thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_buffer): 0 when mode byte +0x190 is 2, otherwise by time-limit byte +0x197: 1 -> 900, 2 -> 1800 (0x708), 3 -> 5400 (0x1518), 4 -> 9000, else 0 (so -1 = no limit). No return value. Side effects: the four timer words. Callers: 3 (tag_duel_handle_partner_swap, duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel, system_duel_mgr_tutorial_reset_callback). |
| `0x08eaac94` | `void duel_core_setup_engine_for_duel(void)` | Prepares rel_duel_eng for the duel described by the setup buffer (deck_get_duel_setup_buffer): clears 0x54 bytes at 0x08eba16c, copies two 3-entry u16 tables from 0x08eb9548 + buffer[0x194] x 0x0c to 0x08eba1a0/0x08eba1a6 and loads the first as the audio track, hides the loading overlay, installs ui_duel_mgr_on_engine_ui_event as the UI event hook, sets the RNG seed (buffer +0x188) and first player (+0x18c), and the local side (offline: duel_core_is_player_on_second_side; online: the network side, 0 when > 1, and marks the net duel running). Mode byte +0x192: 2 (tag duel) maps the four duelists by first player ({0, 3} or {2, 1}) and assigns the decks at +0x230/+0x3dc/+0x588/+0x734, then duel_core_start_duel(1); 1 (single) assigns the decks at +0x230 + side x 0x1ac and calls duel_core_start_duel(0); any other mode returns after the presentation setup. Duelist kinds come from +0x1a4 of each duelist record (1 -> 0, 0 -> 2, other -> 1 for a local or offline side, 2 for the remote side). Then the turn counter from +0x1a0, the face-change hook, both LPs from duel_core_get_setup_start_lp with UI draw command 0x0f, clears the voice queue and presentation flags, 0x08eba02c/0x08eba024, and duel_core_reset_turn_timers. No return value. Side effects: engine setup, presentation state, audio. Callers: 1 (system_duel_mgr_start_duel). |
| `0x08eab240` | `void duel_core_tick_turn_timers(void)` | rel_duel_mgr, every frame: decrements the per-duelist turn timers (words thunk_deck_get_duel_setup_tail_block() + duelist x 4). Offline: for each side, duelist d = thunk_duel_core_map_side_to_duelist(side); when the u16 at set-up buffer +0x1a4 + d x 0x1ac (the duelist record's first u16) is below 2 and the side is awaiting input (thunk_duel_core_is_awaiting_player_input), a positive timer is decremented; then the first of the four duelists whose timer is 0 loses (thunk_duel_core_declare_turn_limit_loss(1) for duelists 1 and 3, (0) for 0 and 2). In a link duel (thunk_system_get_net_active_flag) only duelists whose record u16 +0x0c equals the local peer index are ticked, a timer at 0 declares the loss, and every 30 frames (0x08eba020 % 30) the timer is broadcast (thunk_system_net_api_broadcast_duelist_value). No return value. Side effects: the timers, the loss declaration, the broadcast. Callers: 1 (system_duel_mgr_frame_callback). |
